<h3><a href="https://x.com/AIJonHu/status/2101945213002608907">LangChain evaluates TypeSafe Jev&#39;s cost for Agent scoring</a></h3>
<p>LangChain published an evaluation testing the reliability of TypeSafe&#39;s new model Jev for scoring Agents.
